Generate points deterministically (sequential RNG).\n2. Main loop: while unclustered points remain:\n   - For each unclustered seed, generate candidate cluster (greedy adding closest points maintaining diameter < threshold)\n   - Pick seed with maximum cardinality. Tie-breaking: first seed with strictly greater cardinality wins, iterating in order of unclustered_indices (ascending order initially, then removal preserves order).\n   - Mark members clustered, remove from unclustered.\n\nImportant semantics: tie-breaking matters. In the sequential code, when cardinality > max_cardinality (strict), first encountered wins. The unclustered_indices order is ascending index order (since initialized 0..N-1 and removals preserve order). So the best seed among ties is the smallest index seed. This is important for reproducing exact output hash.\n\nSo candidate cluster generation for each seed is independent given the current `clustered` state. This can be parallelized across seeds. Within a candidate generation, the loop over candidates can be parallelized, but it's iterative (greedy) - each step needs the result of the previous. Distances computed with sqrt in double on GPU should match CPU's sqrt for IEEE double (CUDA sqrt(double) is correctly rounded, same as CPU typically). };\nstruct Cluster { std::vector<int> members; int seed_point; };\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    while (count < N) {\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) group_cnt = N - count;\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) continue;\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++; group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x; double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point, const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n    const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold, const int point_count,\n    std::vector<int>* cluster_members, std::vector<double>& maxd, const double* D) {\n    std::vector<char> in_cluster(point_count, 0);\n    std::vector<int> members; Reduction function maybe inline device.\n\nevaluateSeedsKernel:\n```\n__global__ void evaluateSeedsKernel(const double* __restrict__ D,\n                                    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                    int numSeeds, int N, double threshold,\n                                    double* __restrict__ md_ws,\n                                    int* __restrict__ card_out) {\n    const int s = blockIdx.x;\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nt = blockDim.x;\n    if (s >= numSeeds) return;\n    const int seed = seeds[s];\n    double* md = md_ws + (size_t)s * (size_t)N;\n\n    __shared__ double red_val[256];\n    __shared__ int red_idx[256];\n    __shared__ int chosen;\n\n    for (int c = tid; c < N; c += nt) {\n        md[c] = (clustered[c] != 0) ? CLUSTERED_SENTINEL : D[(size_t)c * (size_t)N + seed];\n    }\n    if (tid == 0) md[seed] = INCLUSTER_SENTINEL;\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int count = 1;\n    for (;;) {\n        double best_val = threshold;\n        int best_idx = -1;\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += nt) {\n            const double v = md[c];\n            if (v >= 0.0 && v < best_val) { best_val = v; best_idx = c; }\n        }\n        red_val[tid] = best_val;\n        red_idx[tid] = best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int offset = nt >> 1; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < offset) {\n                const double v2 = red_val[tid + offset];\n                const int i2 = red_idx[tid + offset];\n                const double v1 = red_val[tid];\n                const int i1 = red_idx[tid];\n                if (v2 < v1 || (v2 == v1 && i2 >= 0 && (i1 < 0 || i2 < i1))) {\n                    red_val[tid] = v2; red_idx[tid] = i2;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n        if (tid == 0) chosen = red_idx[0];\n        __syncthreads();\n        const int m = chosen;\n        if (m < 0) break;\n        if (tid == 0) md[m] = INCLUSTER_SENTINEL;\n        ++count;\n        __syncthreads();\n        const double* col = D + (size_t)m * (size_t)N;\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += nt) {\n            double cur = md[c];\n            if (cur >= 0.0) {\n                const double d = col[c];\n                if (d > cur) md[c] = d;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n    if (tid == 0) card_out[s] = count;\n}\n```\n\nWait: `red_val` initialized to threshold, but if multiple threads have no candidate, v2==v1==threshold, i2=-1. {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792656940990,"sessionID":"ses_e9d57dd07ffen2weNjxGst7qWy","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_00_Vqugy1wb62ru17MxaoVq5040","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/qtclustering_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_hybrid_r4/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering builds clusters by starting from a seed\n// point and iteratively adding the closest point that keeps the cluster's\n// diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI distributes the candidate seeds of every clustering round among\n//     ranks and performs the global best-seed reduction.\n//   * OpenMP builds the distance matrix and drives several independent CUDA\n//     streams per rank.\n//   * CUDA evaluates all candidate clusters of a round in parallel (one\n//     thread block per seed), using an incremental maximum-distance array so\n//     that each greedy step is a linear scan instead of a full recomputation.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <climits>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Sentinels stored in the per-seed max-distance array.\nstatic const double DIST_IN_CLUSTER = -1.0;\nstatic const double DIST_CLUSTERED = -2.0;\n\nstatic int g_device = 0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                         \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);             \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Evaluate the greedy candidate cluster for every seed in one launch.\n// Grid: one thread block per seed. The per-seed \"max distance to any member\"\n// array is updated incrementally: adding a member only ever increases it.\n__global__ void evaluateSeedsKernel(const double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                    const int num_seeds,\n                                    const int point_count,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    double* __restrict__ max_dist,\n                                    int* __restrict__ cardinality) {\n    const int s = blockIdx.x;\n    if (s >= num_seeds) return;\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nt = blockDim.x;\n    const int seed = seeds[s];\n    double* md = max_dist + static_cast<size_t>(s) * static_cast<size_t>(point_count);\n\n    __shared__ double red_val[256];\n    __shared__ int red_idx[256];\n    __shared__ int chosen;\n\n    for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += nt) {\n        md[c] = (clustered[c] != 0)\n                    ? DIST_CLUSTERED\n                    : dist[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + c];\n    }\n    if (tid == 0) md[seed] = DIST_IN_CLUSTER;\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int count = 1;\n    for (;;) {\n        double best_val = threshold;\n        int best_idx = -1;\n        for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += nt) {\n            const double v = md[c];\n            if (v >= 0.0 && v < best_val) {\n                best_val = v;\n                best_idx = c;\n            }\n        }\n\n        red_val[tid] = best_val;\n        red_idx[tid] = best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int offset = nt >> 1;
